   405 | function getHostedWeeksCount(club = {}) {
   406 |   const seasons = Array.isArray(club?.state?.seasons) ? club.state.seasons : [];
   407 | 
   408 |   return seasons.reduce((total, season) => {
   409 |     const history = Array.isArray(season?.matchDayHistory)
   410 |       ? season.matchDayHistory
   411 |       : [];
   412 | 
   413 |     return total + history.length;
   414 |   }, 0);
   415 | }

   425 |     activityCount: Number.isFinite(Number(club.activityCount)) ? Number(club.activityCount) : 0,
   426 |     hostedWeeksCount,
   427 |   };

   434 | function challengeFixtureTimestamp(fixture = {}) {
   435 |   const date = String(
   436 |     fixture?.confirmedDate ||
   437 |     fixture?.proposedDate ||
   438 |     fixture?.matchDate ||
   439 |     ""
   440 |   ).trim();
   441 | 
   442 |   const kickoff = String(
   443 |     fixture?.confirmedKickoff ||
   444 |     fixture?.proposedKickoff ||
   445 |     fixture?.kickoff ||
   446 |     "00:00"
   447 |   ).trim();
   448 | 
   449 |   if (!date) {
   450 |     return Number(fixture?.createdAtMs || fixture?.updatedAtMs || 0);
   451 |   }
   452 | 
   453 |   const parsed = new Date(`${date}T${kickoff || "00:00"}`);
   454 | 
   455 |   return Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())
   456 |     ? Number(fixture?.createdAtMs || fixture?.updatedAtMs || 0)
   457 |     : parsed.getTime();
   458 | }

   460 | function formatChallengeFixtureDate(fixture = {}) {
   461 |   const date = String(
   462 |     fixture?.confirmedDate ||
   463 |     fixture?.proposedDate ||
   464 |     fixture?.matchDate ||
   465 |     ""
   466 |   ).trim();
   467 | 
   468 |   if (!date) return "Date to be confirmed";
   469 | 
   470 |   const parsed = new Date(`${date}T12:00:00`);
   471 | 
   472 |   if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) return date;
   473 | 
   474 |   return parsed.toLocaleDateString("en-ZA", {
   475 |     weekday: "short",
   476 |     day: "numeric",
   477 |     month: "short",
   478 |     year: "numeric",
   479 |   });
   480 | }

   504 | function challengeFixtureIsComplete(fixture = {}) {
   505 |   const status = challengeFixtureStatusKey(fixture);
   506 | 
   507 |   return (
   508 |     ["completed", "complete", "finished", "full_time", "full-time", "final"].includes(status) ||
   509 |     Boolean(fixture?.finishedAt || fixture?.completedAt) ||
   510 |     (
   511 |       Number.isFinite(Number(fixture?.homeScore)) &&
   512 |       Number.isFinite(Number(fixture?.awayScore)) &&
   513 |       status === "result_recorded"
   514 |     )
   515 |   );
   516 | }
   517 | 
   518 | function challengeFixtureIsLive(fixture = {}) {
   519 |   const status = challengeFixtureStatusKey(fixture);
   520 | 
   521 |   return ["live", "in_progress", "in-progress", "started"].includes(status);
   522 | }
   523 | 
   524 | function challengeFixtureIsRequest(fixture = {}) {
   525 |   const status = challengeFixtureStatusKey(fixture);
   526 | 
   527 |   return [
   528 |     "pending",
   529 |     "change_requested",
   530 |     "change-requested",
   531 |     "awaiting_confirmation",
   532 |     "awaiting-confirmation",
   533 |   ].includes(status);

   536 | function getChallengeFixtureFormatLabel(value = "") {
   537 |   const format = String(value || "5v5").trim().toLowerCase();
   538 | 
   539 |   const labels = {
   540 |     "5v5": "5-a-side",
   541 |     "6v6": "6-a-side",
   542 |     "7v7": "7-a-side",
   543 |     "11v11": "11-a-side",
   544 |   };
   545 | 
   546 |   return labels[format] || String(value || "5-a-side");
   547 | }

   549 | function getChallengeClubInitials(name = "") {
   550 |   const parts = String(name || "Club")
   551 |     .trim()
   552 |     .split(/\s+/)
   553 |     .filter(Boolean);
   554 | 
   555 |   if (!parts.length) return "FC";
   556 | 
   557 |   return parts
   558 |     .slice(0, 2)
   559 |     .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase())
   560 |     .join("");
   561 | }

   647 |     async function loadClubs() {
   648 |       try {
   649 |         setLoadingClubs(true);
   650 | 
   651 |         const snap = await getDocs(collection(db, "clubs"));
   652 |         const firebaseClubsRaw = snap.docs
   653 |           .map(normalizeClub)
   654 |           .filter((club) => {
   655 |             const status = String(club?.status || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   656 |             return status !== "deleted" && club?.deleted !== true;
   657 |           });
   658 |         const firebaseClubs = await Promise.all(
   659 |           firebaseClubsRaw.map(async (club) => {
   660 |             const hydratedClub = hydrateClubHubStats(club);
   661 |             const clubId = String(hydratedClub?.id || hydratedClub?.clubId || "").trim();
   662 | 
   663 |             if (!clubId) return hydratedClub;
   664 | 
   665 |             try {
   666 |               const membersSnap = await getDocs(collection(db, "clubs", clubId, "members"));
   667 |               return {
   668 |                 ...hydratedClub,
   669 |                 playerCount: membersSnap.size,
   670 |               };
   671 |             } catch (error) {
   672 |               console.warn("[HomePage_HUB] Could not count club members:", clubId, error);
   673 |               return hydratedClub;
   674 |             }
   675 |           })
   676 |         );
   677 | 
   678 |         if (!cancelled) {
   679 |           const nextClubs = firebaseClubs.length ? firebaseClubs : FALLBACK_CLUBS;
   680 |           setClubs(nextClubs);
   681 | 
   682 |           try {
   683 |             window.localStorage.setItem(CLUB_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(nextClubs));
   684 |           } catch {
   685 |             // Ignore cache failures.
   686 |           }
   687 |         }
   688 |       } catch (error) {
   689 |         console.error("[HomePage_HUB] Failed to load clubs:", error);
   690 |         if (!cancelled) setClubs(FALLBACK_CLUBS);
   691 |       } finally {
   692 |         if (!cancelled) setLoadingClubs(false);
   693 |       }
   694 |     }
   695 | 
   696 |     loadClubs();
   697 | 
   698 |     return () => {
   699 |       cancelled = true;
   700 |     };
   701 |   }, []);

   883 |   async function handleDeleteClub(club) {
   884 |     const clubId = String(club?.id || club?.clubId || "").trim();
   885 | 
   886 |     if (!isSuperAdmin(currentUser)) {
   887 |       window.alert("Only the super admin can delete clubs.");
   888 |       return;
   889 |     }
   890 | 
   891 |     if (!clubId) {
   892 |       window.alert("Could not identify this club.");
   893 |       return;
   894 |     }
   895 | 
   896 |     const clubName = club?.name || clubId;
   897 |     const typed = window.prompt(
   898 |       `Type DELETE to remove ${clubName} from the public hub. This will hide the club but keep a record for audit purposes.`
   899 |     );
   900 | 
   901 |     if (typed !== "DELETE") return;
   902 | 
   903 |     try {
   904 |       setDeletingClubId(clubId);
   905 | 
   906 |       await updateDoc(doc(db, "clubs", clubId), {
   907 |         status: "deleted",
   908 |         deleted: true,
   909 |         deletedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   910 |         deletedByEmail: currentUser?.email || "",
   911 |         deletedByUid: currentUser?.uid || "",
   912 |       });
   913 | 
   914 |       setClubs((current) => {
   915 |         const next = current.filter((item) => String(item?.id || item?.clubId || "") !== clubId);
   916 | 
   917 |         try {
   918 |           window.localStorage.setItem(CLUB_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
   919 |         } catch {
   920 |           // Ignore cache failures.
   921 |         }
   922 | 
   923 |         return next;
   924 |       });
   925 | 
   926 |       setActiveClub(null);
   927 |       setActiveMapClub(null);
   928 |     } catch (error) {
   929 |       console.error("[HomePage_HUB] Could not delete club:", error);
   930 |       window.alert("Could not delete this club. Please try again.");
   931 |     } finally {
   932 |       setDeletingClubId("");
   933 |     }
   934 |   }

  1969 |   function isChallengerChatActive(fixture = {}) {
  1970 |     const status = String(fixture?.status || "").toLowerCase();
  1971 | 
  1972 |     if (
  1973 |       status.includes("cancel") ||
  1974 |       status.includes("rejected") ||
  1975 |       status.includes("declined")
  1976 |     ) {
  1977 |       return false;
  1978 |     }
  1979 | 
  1980 |     const dateText = String(fixture?.proposedDate || "").trim();
  1981 |     if (!dateText) return true;
  1982 | 
  1983 |     const kickoffText = String(fixture?.proposedKickoff || "23:59").trim();
  1984 |     const timeText = /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(kickoffText) ? kickoffText : "23:59";
  1985 |     const fixtureTime = new Date(`${dateText}T${timeText}:00`);
  1986 | 
  1987 |     if (Number.isNaN(fixtureTime.getTime())) return true;
  1988 | 
  1989 |     return fixtureTime.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 >= Date.now();
  1990 |   }

  1969 |   function isChallengerChatActive(fixture = {}) {
  1970 |     const status = String(fixture?.status || "").toLowerCase();
  1971 | 
  1972 |     if (
  1973 |       status.includes("cancel") ||
  1974 |       status.includes("rejected") ||
  1975 |       status.includes("declined")
  1976 |     ) {
  1977 |       return false;
  1978 |     }
  1979 | 
  1980 |     const dateText = String(fixture?.proposedDate || "").trim();
  1981 |     if (!dateText) return true;
  1982 | 
  1983 |     const kickoffText = String(fixture?.proposedKickoff || "23:59").trim();
  1984 |     const timeText = /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(kickoffText) ? kickoffText : "23:59";
  1985 |     const fixtureTime = new Date(`${dateText}T${timeText}:00`);
  1986 | 
  1987 |     if (Number.isNaN(fixtureTime.getTime())) return true;
  1988 | 
  1989 |     return fixtureTime.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 >= Date.now();
  1990 |   }

  2610 |   const acceptPlatformPairingChallenge = async (challenge) => {
  2611 |     const pairingId = String(
  2612 |       challenge?.platformPairingId ||
  2613 |       challenge?.challengeId ||
  2614 |       ""
  2615 |     ).trim();
  2616 | 
  2617 |     if (!pairingId || !activeClubId) return;
  2618 | 
  2619 |     const centralRef = doc(db, "clubChallenges", pairingId);
  2620 | 
  2621 |     try {
  2622 |       await runTransaction(db, async (transaction) => {
  2623 |         const centralSnap = await transaction.get(centralRef);
  2624 | 
  2625 |         const pairing = {
  2626 |           ...(challenge || {}),
  2627 |           ...(centralSnap.exists()
  2628 |             ? centralSnap.data()
  2629 |             : {}),
  2630 |         };
  2631 | 
  2632 |         const homeClubId = String(
  2633 |           pairing?.homeClubId ||
  2634 |           pairing?.challengerClubId ||
  2635 |           ""
  2636 |         ).trim();
  2637 | 
  2638 |         const awayClubId = String(
  2639 |           pairing?.awayClubId ||
  2640 |           pairing?.targetClubId ||
  2641 |           ""
  2642 |         ).trim();
  2643 | 
  2644 |         if (
  2645 |           !homeClubId ||
  2646 |           !awayClubId ||
  2647 |           ![homeClubId, awayClubId].includes(activeClubId)
  2648 |         ) {
  2649 |           throw new Error(
  2650 |             "Current club is not part of this platform pairing."
  2651 |           );
  2652 |         }
  2653 | 
  2654 |         const homeClubName =
  2655 |           pairing?.homeClubName ||
  2656 |           pairing?.challengerClubName ||
  2657 |           "Home Club";
  2658 | 
  2659 |         const awayClubName =
  2660 |           pairing?.awayClubName ||
  2661 |           pairing?.targetClubName ||
  2662 |           "Away Club";
  2663 | 
  2664 |         const previousResponses = {
  2665 |           ...(pairing?.responses || {}),
  2666 |         };
  2667 | 
  2668 |         const nextResponses = {
  2669 |           ...previousResponses,
  2670 |           [activeClubId]: "accepted",
  2671 |         };
  2672 | 
  2673 |         const homeAccepted =
  2674 |           nextResponses[homeClubId] === "accepted";
  2675 | 
  2676 |         const awayAccepted =
  2677 |           nextResponses[awayClubId] === "accepted";
  2678 | 
  2679 |         const acceptedClubIds = [
  2680 |           homeAccepted ? homeClubId : "",
  2681 |           awayAccepted ? awayClubId : "",
  2682 |         ].filter(Boolean);
  2683 | 
  2684 |         const bothAccepted =
  2685 |           homeAccepted && awayAccepted;
  2686 | 
  2687 |         const fixtureId = `challenge_${pairingId}`;
  2688 |         const nowMs = Date.now();
  2689 | 
  2690 |         const responsePatch = {
  2691 |           responses: nextResponses,
  2692 |           acceptedClubIds,
  2693 |           homeAccepted,
  2694 |           awayAccepted,
  2695 | 
  2696 |           status: bothAccepted
  2697 |             ? "accepted"
  2698 |             : "awaiting_other_club",
  2699 | 
  2700 |           fixtureStatus: bothAccepted
  2701 |             ? "fixture_created_automatically"
  2702 |             : "awaiting_both_clubs",
  2703 | 
  2704 |           updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  2705 |           updatedAtMs: nowMs,
  2706 | 
  2707 |           ...(bothAccepted ? { fixtureId } : {}),
  2708 |         };
  2709 | 
  2710 |         transaction.set(
  2711 |           centralRef,
  2712 |           responsePatch,
  2713 |           { merge: true }
  2714 |         );
  2715 | 
  2716 |         transaction.set(
  2717 |           doc(
  2718 |             db,
  2719 |             "clubs",
  2720 |             activeClubId,
  2721 |             "incomingChallenges",
  2722 |             pairingId
  2723 |           ),
  2724 |           {
  2725 |             ...responsePatch,
  2726 |             respondedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  2727 |             respondedAtMs: nowMs,
  2728 |           },
  2729 |           { merge: true }
  2730 |         );
  2731 | 
  2732 |         if (!bothAccepted) {
  2733 |           return;
  2734 |         }
  2735 | 
  2736 |         const participatingClubIds = [
  2737 |           homeClubId,
  2738 |           awayClubId,
  2739 |         ];
  2740 | 
  2741 |         const fixturePayload = {
  2742 |           fixtureId,
  2743 | 
  2744 |           source: "platform_pairing",
  2745 |           challengeId: pairingId,
  2746 |           platformPairingId: pairingId,
  2747 |           platformInstigated: true,
  2748 | 
  2749 |           status: "confirmed",
  2750 |           signupStatus: "open",
  2751 | 
  2752 |           homeClubId,

  2755 |             pairing?.homeClubLogo ||
  2756 |             pairing?.challengerClubLogo ||

  2800 |           updatedAtMs: nowMs,

  3003 |   const handleAcceptChallenge = async (challenge) => {

  3005 |       challenge?.source === "platform_pairing" ||
  3006 |       challenge?.platformPairingId
  3007 |     ) {

  3011 | 
  3012 |     if (!challenge?.challengeId) return;
  3013 |

  3015 |       const fixtureId =

  3018 |           challenge.challengeId || Date.now()
  3019 |         ).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_")}`;

  3166 |     } catch (err) {

   194 | function toTitleCase(name) {
   195 |   return String(name || "")
   196 |     .trim()
   197 |     .toLowerCase()
   198 |     .split(/\s+/)
   199 |     .filter(Boolean)
   200 |     .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1))
   201 |     .join(" ");
   202 | }
   203 | 
   204 | function slugFromName(name) {
   205 |   return String(name || "")
   206 |     .toLowerCase()
   207 |     .replace(/\s+/g, "_")
   208 |     .replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, "");
   209 | }
   210 | 
   211 | 
   212 | function normalizeWhatsAppNumber(value) {
   213 |   const raw = String(value || "").trim();
   214 |   if (!raw) return "";
   215 |   if (raw.startsWith("+")) {
   216 |     return "+" + raw.slice(1).replace(/\D/g, "");
   217 |   }
   218 |   return raw.replace(/\D/g, "");
   219 | }
   220 | 
   221 | function looksLikeWhatsAppNumber(value) {
   222 |   const normalized = normalizeWhatsAppNumber(value);
   223 |   const digits = normalized.replace(/\D/g, "");
   224 |   return digits.length >= 9 && digits.length <= 15;
   225 | }
   226 | 
   227 | 
   228 | function fileToDataUrl(file) {
   229 |   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   230 |     const reader = new FileReader();
   231 |     reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result);
   232 |     reader.onerror = reject;
   233 |     reader.readAsDataURL(file);
   234 |   });
   235 | }
   236 | 
   237 | async function makeLandscapePhotoDataUrl(file, width = 900, height = 506) {
   238 |   const raw = await fileToDataUrl(file);
   239 | 
   240 |   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   241 |     const img = new Image();
   242 |     img.onload = () => {
   243 |       try {
   244 |         const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
   245 |         canvas.width = width;
   246 |         canvas.height = height;
   247 |         const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
   248 | 
   249 |         if (!ctx) {
   250 |           resolve(raw);
   251 |           return;
   252 |         }
   253 | 
   254 |         ctx.fillStyle = "#020617";
   255 |         ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
   256 | 
   257 |         const scale = Math.max(width / img.width, height / img.height);
   258 |         const drawW = img.width * scale;
   259 |         const drawH = img.height * scale;
   260 |         const dx = (width - drawW) / 2;
   261 |         const dy = (height - drawH) / 2;
   262 | 
   263 |         ctx.drawImage(img, dx, dy, drawW, drawH);
   264 |         resolve(canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.72));
   265 |       } catch (err) {
   266 |         reject(err);
   267 |       }
   268 |     };
   269 |     img.onerror = reject;
   270 |     img.src = raw;
   271 |   });
   272 | }

   436 |     );

   438 |   }
   439 | }

   441 | async function resolveSignedInRoleFromPlayerDoc(member, emailFromGoogle = "", clubId = DEFAULT_CLUB_ID) {
   442 |   const shortName = toTitleCase(member?.shortName || "");
   443 |   const fullName = toTitleCase(member?.fullName || "");
   444 |   const displayName = toTitleCase(shortName || fullName);
   445 | 
   446 |   const candidateIds = Array.from(
   447 |     new Set(
   448 |       [
   449 |         slugFromName(displayName),
   450 |         slugFromName(fullName),
   451 |         slugFromName(shortName),
   452 |         member?.playerId || "",
   453 |       ].filter(Boolean)
   454 |     )
   455 |   );
   456 | 
   457 |   for (const pid of candidateIds) {
   458 |     try {
   459 |       const snap = await getDoc(playerDocRef(clubId, pid));
   460 |       if (!snap.exists()) continue;
   461 | 
   462 |       const data = snap.data() || {};
   463 |       const roles = data.roles || {};
   464 | 
   465 |       if (roles.admin) return "admin";
   466 |       if (roles.captain) return "captain";
   467 |       if (roles.player) return "player";
   468 |     } catch (err) {
   469 |       console.error("[EntryPage] Failed reading player role:", err);
   470 |     }
   471 |   }
   472 | 
   473 |   const email = String(emailFromGoogle || member?.email || "")
   474 |     .trim()
   475 |     .toLowerCase();
   476 | 
   477 |   if (isCaptainEmail(email)) return "captain";
   478 |   return "player";
   479 | }

   491 | }

   493 | async function clearWithdrawnPlayerPrivateDetails({ clubId = DEFAULT_CLUB_ID, memberId = "", playerId = "" }) {
   494 |   if (memberId) {
   495 |     await updateDoc(
   496 |       memberDocRef(clubId, memberId),
   497 |       getPrivateContactDeletePayload({
   498 |         status: "withdrawn",
   499 |         updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   500 |       })
   501 |     );
   502 |   }
   503 | 
   504 |   if (playerId) {
   505 |     try {
   506 |       await updateDoc(
   507 |         playerDocRef(clubId, playerId),
   508 |         getPrivateContactDeletePayload({
   509 |           updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   510 |         })
   511 |       );
   512 |     } catch (err) {
   513 |       console.error("[EntryPage] Could not clear player contact details:", err);
   514 |     }
   515 |   }

  1969 |   function isChallengerChatActive(fixture = {}) {
  1970 |     const status = String(fixture?.status || "").toLowerCase();
  1971 | 
  1972 |     if (
  1973 |       status.includes("cancel") ||
  1974 |       status.includes("rejected") ||
  1975 |       status.includes("declined")
  1976 |     ) {
  1977 |       return false;
  1978 |     }
  1979 | 
  1980 |     const dateText = String(fixture?.proposedDate || "").trim();
  1981 |     if (!dateText) return true;
  1982 | 
  1983 |     const kickoffText = String(fixture?.proposedKickoff || "23:59").trim();
  1984 |     const timeText = /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(kickoffText) ? kickoffText : "23:59";
  1985 |     const fixtureTime = new Date(`${dateText}T${timeText}:00`);
  1986 | 
  1987 |     if (Number.isNaN(fixtureTime.getTime())) return true;
  1988 | 
  1989 |     return fixtureTime.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 >= Date.now();

    63 | function toTitleCase(name) {
    64 |   return String(name || "")
    65 |     .trim()
    66 |     .toLowerCase()
    67 |     .split(/\s+/)
    68 |     .filter(Boolean)
    69 |     .map((w) => w.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + w.slice(1))
    70 |     .join(" ");
    71 | }
    72 | 
    73 | function slugFromName(name) {
    74 |   return toTitleCase(name)
    75 |     .toLowerCase()
    76 |     .replace(/\s+/g, "_")
    77 |     .replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, "");
    78 | }
    79 | 
    80 | function normalizeAbbrev(value) {
    81 |   return String(value || "")
    82 |     .toUpperCase()
    83 |     .replace(/[^A-Z]/g, "")
    84 |     .slice(0, 4);
    85 | }
    86 | 
    87 | function isValidAbbrev(value) {
    88 |   const clean = String(value || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    89 |   return /^[A-Z]{3,4}$/.test(clean);
    90 | }
    91 | 
    92 | function normalizeHexColor(v) {
    93 |   const raw = String(v || "").trim().replace(/[^#a-fA-F0-9]/g, "");
    94 |   if (!raw) return "";
    95 |   if (/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(raw)) return raw.toUpperCase();
    96 |   if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(raw)) return `#${raw.toUpperCase()}`;
    97 |   return raw.toUpperCase();
    98 | }
    99 | 
   100 | function isValidHexColor(v) {
   101 |   return /^#[0-9A-F]{6}$/.test(String(v || "").trim().toUpperCase());
   102 | }

   151 | function bestFullDisplayFromPlayer(p) {
   152 |   if (!p) return "";
   153 |   const fullName = toTitleCase(p.fullName || "");
   154 |   if (fullName) return fullName;
   155 | 
   156 |   const aliasesArr = Array.isArray(p.aliases) ? p.aliases : [];
   157 |   const aliasCandidates = aliasesArr
   158 |     .map((a) => toTitleCase(a))
   159 |     .filter(Boolean)
   160 |     .sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length);
   161 | 
   162 |   if (aliasCandidates.length) return aliasCandidates[0];
   163 | 
   164 |   const shortName = toTitleCase(p.shortName || "");
   165 |   if (shortName) return shortName;
   166 | 
   167 |   const name = toTitleCase(p.name || "");
   168 |   if (name) return name;
   169 | 
   170 |   return toTitleCase(p.id || "");
   171 | }
   172 | 
   173 | function bestShortDisplayFromPlayer(p) {
   174 |   if (!p) return "";
   175 |   const shortName = toTitleCase(p.shortName || "");
   176 |   if (shortName) return shortName;
   177 | 
   178 |   const name = toTitleCase(p.name || "");
   179 |   if (name) return name;
   180 | 
   181 |   const fullName = toTitleCase(p.fullName || "");
   182 |   if (fullName) return fullName;
   183 | 
   184 |   return toTitleCase(p.id || "");
   185 | }

   187 | function buildIdentityStrings(playerDoc) {
   188 |   const id = String(playerDoc.id || "").trim();
   189 |   const name = toTitleCase(playerDoc.name || "");
   190 |   const fullName = toTitleCase(playerDoc.fullName || "");
   191 |   const shortName = toTitleCase(playerDoc.shortName || "");
   192 |   const aliasesArr = Array.isArray(playerDoc.aliases) ? playerDoc.aliases : [];
   193 |   const aliases = aliasesArr.map((a) => toTitleCase(a));
   194 | 
   195 |   const strings = [id, name, fullName, shortName, ...aliases].filter(Boolean);
   196 |   return Array.from(new Set(strings.map((s) => s.toLowerCase())));
   197 | }

   199 | function resolvePlayerIdFromString(allPlayers, raw) {
   200 |   const needle = toTitleCase(raw).toLowerCase();
   201 |   if (!needle) return null;
   202 | 
   203 |   const direct = allPlayers.find((p) => String(p.id).toLowerCase() === needle);
   204 |   if (direct) return direct.id;
   205 | 
   206 |   for (const p of allPlayers) {
   207 |     const candidates = buildIdentityStrings(p);
   208 |     if (candidates.includes(needle)) return p.id;
   209 |   }
   210 |   return null;
   211 | }

  1053 |             status.includes("complete") ||

  1066 |           const bTime = new Date(`${b.proposedDate}T12:00:00`).getTime();
  1067 |           return aTime - bTime;
  1068 |         });

  1071 |         sortedFixtures.find((fixture) => {
  1072 |           const fixtureDate = new Date(`${fixture.proposedDate}T12:00:00`);

  1074 |         }) || null;

  1102 |           ...hydratedFixture,
  1103 |           homeClubLogo,
  1104 |           awayClubLogo,

  1356 | 
  1357 |   const seasonPlayerTeamIdByPlayerId = useMemo(() => {
  1358 |     if (!isLeague) return new Map();
  1359 | 
  1360 |     const teamLookup = new Map();
  1361 |     (localLeagueTeams || []).forEach((team) => {
  1362 |       const id = String(team?.id || "").trim();
  1363 |       const label = String(team?.label || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  1364 |       const abbrev = String(team?.abbrev || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  1365 | 
  1366 |       if (id) teamLookup.set(id.toLowerCase(), id);
  1367 |       if (label) teamLookup.set(label, id);
  1368 |       if (abbrev) teamLookup.set(abbrev, id);
  1369 |     });
  1370 | 
  1371 |     const out = new Map();
  1372 | 
  1373 |     (Array.isArray(matchDayHistory) ? matchDayHistory : []).forEach((day) => {
  1374 |       const entries = Array.isArray(day?.playerAppearances)
  1375 |         ? day.playerAppearances

  1403 |           String(rawTeam || "").trim();

  1406 |           out.set(playerId, resolvedTeamId);

  1413 | 
  1414 |   useEffect(() => {

  1417 | 
  1418 |     setLocalLeagueTeams((prevTeams) => {
  1419 |       const alreadyAssigned = new Set(
  1420 |         (prevTeams || []).flatMap((team) =>
  1421 |           Array.isArray(team?.players) ? team.players : []
  1422 |         )
  1423 |       );
  1424 | 
  1425 |       const playersToRestore = Array.from(seasonPlayerTeamIdByPlayerId.entries())
  1426 |         .filter(([playerId]) => playersById.has(playerId) && !alreadyAssigned.has(playerId));
  1427 | 
  1428 |       if (!playersToRestore.length) return prevTeams;
  1429 | 
  1430 |       return (prevTeams || []).map((team) => {
  1431 |         const addHere = playersToRestore
  1432 |           .filter(([, teamId]) => teamId === team.id)
  1433 |           .map(([playerId]) => playerId);
  1434 | 
  1435 |         if (!addHere.length) return team;
  1436 | 
  1437 |         return {
  1438 |           ...team,
  1439 |           players: Array.from(new Set([...(team.players || []), ...addHere])),
  1440 |         };
  1441 |       });
  1442 |     });

  1444 | 
  1445 | 
  1446 |   useEffect(() => {
  1447 |     if (!allPlayers.length) return;

  1449 |     const normalizeTeamsAgainstPlayers = (prevTeams) =>
  1450 |       prevTeams.map((t) => {
  1451 |         const nextPlayers = (t.players || []).map((entry) => {
  1452 |           if (playersById.has(entry)) return entry;
  1453 |           const resolved = resolvePlayerIdFromString(allPlayers, entry);
  1454 |           return resolved || entry;
  1455 |         });
  1456 | 
  1457 |         const seen = new Set();
  1458 |         const deduped = [];
  1459 |         for (const x of nextPlayers) {
  1460 |           if (!x) continue;
  1461 |           if (seen.has(x)) continue;
  1462 |           seen.add(x);
  1463 |           deduped.push(x);
  1464 |         }
  1465 | 
  1466 |         let captainId = t.captainId || null;
  1467 |         if (!captainId && t.captain) {
  1468 |           const resolvedCaptain = resolvePlayerIdFromString(allPlayers, t.captain);
  1469 |           if (resolvedCaptain) captainId = resolvedCaptain;
  1470 |         }
  1471 | 
  1472 |         return { ...t, players: deduped, captainId };
  1473 |       });
  1474 | 
  1475 |     setLocalLeagueTeams((prev) => normalizeTeamsAgainstPlayers(prev));
  1476 |     setLocalFiveVFiveTeams((prev) => normalizeTeamsAgainstPlayers(prev));
  1477 |   }, [allPlayers, playersById]);
  1478 | 
  1479 |   const baseFriendlyTeams = useMemo(() => {
  1480 |     const normalized = normalizeIncomingTeams(localFiveVFiveTeams);
  1481 | 
  1482 |     if (isPracticeMode) {

  2009 |   const confirmLeagueIdentityChange = (teamId, fieldLabel) => {
  2010 |     if (!isLeague) return true;
  2011 | 
  2012 |     const key = `${teamId}:${fieldLabel}`;
  2013 |     if (leagueIdentityConfirmRef.current[key]) return true;
  2014 | 
  2015 |     const ok = window.confirm(
  2016 |       `You are changing the ${fieldLabel} during an active League season. ` +
  2017 |       `This should normally be set once per season. Continue?`
  2018 |     );
  2019 | 
  2020 |     if (ok) {
  2021 |       leagueIdentityConfirmRef.current[key] = true;
  2022 |     }
  2023 | 
  2024 |     return ok;
  2025 |   };
  2026 | 
  2027 |   const baseAbbrevFromName = (name) => {
  2028 |     const cleanName = String(name || "").trim();
  2029 |     const upper = cleanName.toUpperCase();
  2030 |     const words = upper.replace(/[^A-Z0-9 ]/g, " ").split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  2031 | 
  2032 |     if (!words.length) return "";
  2033 | 
  2034 |     const compact = words.join("");
  2035 | 
  2036 |     if (/^DARK/.test(compact)) return "DARK";
  2037 |     if (/^LIGHT/.test(compact)) return "LIGT";
  2038 | 
  2039 |     if (words[0] === "MAN" || words[0] === "MANCHESTER") {
  2040 |       const second = words[1] || "";
  2041 |       if (second.startsWith("C")) return "MCI";
  2042 |       if (second.startsWith("U")) return "MUN";
  2043 |     }
  2044 | 
  2045 |     if (compact.startsWith("MADRID")) return "MAD";
  2046 |     if (compact.startsWith("BARCELONA")) return "BAR";
  2047 |     if (compact.startsWith("AJAX")) return "AJA";
  2048 | 
  2049 |     if (words.length >= 2) {
  2050 |       const initials = words.map((word) => word[0]).join("");
  2051 |       if (initials.length >= 3) return initials.slice(0, 4);
  2052 |     }
  2053 | 
  2054 |     return compact.slice(0, compact.length >= 4 ? 4 : 3);
  2055 |   };
  2056 | 
  2057 |   const makeUniqueTeamAbbrev = (teamId, nextName, teamsList = sourceTeams) => {
  2058 |     const preferred = normalizeAbbrev(baseAbbrevFromName(nextName));
  2059 |     if (!preferred) return "";
  2060 | 
  2061 |     const taken = new Set(
  2062 |       (teamsList || [])
  2063 |         .filter((team) => team.id !== teamId)
  2064 |         .map((team) => normalizeAbbrev(team.abbrev || baseAbbrevFromName(team.label)))
  2065 |         .filter(Boolean)
  2066 |     );
  2067 | 
  2068 |     if (!taken.has(preferred)) return preferred;
  2069 | 
  2070 |     const compact = String(nextName || "").toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g, "");
  2071 |     const candidates = [
  2072 |       compact.slice(0, 4),
  2073 |       compact.slice(0, 3),
  2074 |       ...compact.split("").map((_, index) => `${preferred.slice(0, 3)}${compact[index] || ""}`),
  2075 |     ]
  2076 |       .map(normalizeAbbrev)
  2077 |       .filter((value) => value.length >= 3 && value.length <= 4);
  2078 | 
  2079 |     return candidates.find((candidate) => candidate && !taken.has(candidate)) || preferred;
  2080 |   };
  2081 |

  2209 |   const handleCaptainChange = (teamId, captainId) => {

  2229 |   const handleAddPlayer = async (id) => {
  2230 |     if (!canEdit) return;
  2231 |     const raw = pendingNames[id] || "";
  2232 |     const trimmed = raw.trim();
  2233 |     if (!trimmed) return;
  2234 | 
  2235 |     const targetTeam = sourceTeams.find((team) => team.id === id);
  2236 | 
  2237 |     if (targetTeam && isCurrentGuestOpponentTeam(targetTeam)) {
  2238 |       const selectedId = parseChoiceToPlayerId(trimmed);
  2239 |       const selectedName = playersById.has(selectedId)
  2240 |         ? displayNameOf(selectedId)
  2241 |         : toTitleCase(
  2242 |             trimmed.includes("|")
  2243 |               ? trimmed.split("|").slice(1).join("|")
  2244 |               : selectedId
  2245 |           );
  2246 | 
  2247 |       if (!selectedName) return;
  2248 | 
  2249 |       setGuestOpponentPlayers((prev) => {
  2250 |         const existing = new Set((prev || []).map((p) => toTitleCase(p).toLowerCase()));
  2251 |         if (existing.has(selectedName.toLowerCase())) return prev;
  2252 |         return [...(prev || []), selectedName];
  2253 |       });
  2254 | 
  2255 |       setPendingNames((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: "" }));
  2256 |       setAddErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: "" }));
  2257 |       return;
  2258 |     }
  2259 | 
  2260 |     let chosenId = parseChoiceToPlayerId(trimmed);
  2261 |     if (!playersById.has(chosenId)) {
  2262 |       const resolved = resolvePlayerIdFromString(allPlayers, chosenId);
  2263 |       if (resolved) {
  2264 |         chosenId = resolved;
  2265 |       } else {
  2266 |         setAddErrors((prev) => ({
  2267 |           ...prev,
  2268 |           [id]: "Select an existing club player. New players must join the club first.",
  2269 |         }));
  2270 |         return;
  2271 |       }
  2272 |     }
  2273 | 
  2274 |     const teamIndex = sourceTeams.findIndex((t) =>
  2275 |       (t.players || []).some((pid) => pid === chosenId)
  2276 |     );
  2277 |     const inAnyTeam = teamIndex >= 0;
  2278 | 
  2279 |     if (id === UNSEEDED_ID) {
  2280 |       if (!inAnyTeam) {
  2281 |         setAddErrors((prev) => ({
  2282 |           ...prev,
  2283 |           [id]: `${displayNameOf(chosenId)} is already unseeded.`,
  2284 |         }));
  2285 |         return;
  2286 |       }
  2287 | 
  2288 |       setSourceTeams((prev) =>
  2289 |         prev.map((t, idx) => {
  2290 |           if (idx !== teamIndex) return t;
  2291 |           const nextPlayers = (t.players || []).filter((pid) => pid !== chosenId);
  2292 |           const nextCaptainId = t.captainId === chosenId ? null : t.captainId;
  2293 |           return { ...t, players: nextPlayers, captainId: nextCaptainId };
  2294 |         })
  2295 |       );
  2296 |     } else {
  2297 |       const targetIndex = sourceTeams.findIndex((t) => t.id === id);
  2298 |       if (targetIndex === -1) {
  2299 |         setAddErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: "Unknown team." }));
  2300 |         return;
  2301 |       }
  2302 | 
  2303 |       const targetTeam = sourceTeams[targetIndex];
  2304 |       if ((targetTeam.players || []).includes(chosenId)) {
  2305 |         setAddErrors((prev) => ({
  2306 |           ...prev,
  2307 |           [id]: `${displayNameOf(chosenId)} is already in this team.`,
  2308 |         }));
  2309 |         return;
  2310 |       }
  2311 | 
  2312 |       if (inAnyTeam && teamIndex !== targetIndex) {
  2313 |         const existingTeam = sourceTeams[teamIndex];
  2314 |         setAddErrors((prev) => ({
  2315 |           ...prev,
  2316 |           [id]: `${displayNameOf(chosenId)} is already in ${existingTeam.label}. Move them to Unseeded first, then assign to this team.`,
  2317 |         }));
  2318 |         return;
  2319 |       }
  2320 | 
  2321 |       setSourceTeams((prev) =>
  2322 |         prev.map((t, idx) =>
  2323 |           idx === targetIndex ? { ...t, players: [...(t.players || []), chosenId] } : t
  2324 |         )
  2325 |       );
  2326 |     }
  2327 | 
  2328 |     setPendingNames((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: "" }));
  2329 |     setAddErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: "" }));
  2330 |   };
  2331 | 
  2332 |   const handleRemovePlayer = async (teamId, playerIdOrLegacy) => {

  2474 |   const validateTeams = (candidateTeams) => {
  2475 |     const badAbbrev = candidateTeams.find((t) => t.abbrev && !isValidAbbrev(t.abbrev));
  2476 |     if (badAbbrev) {
  2477 |       return `Invalid abbreviation for "${badAbbrev.label || badAbbrev.id}". Use 3 or 4 letters (A–Z).`;

  2482 |       return `Invalid team color for "${badColor.label || badColor.id}". Use hex like #DC2626`;

  2485 |     const abbrevs = candidateTeams.map((t) => t.abbrev).filter(Boolean);
  2486 |     const dup = abbrevs.find((a, i) => abbrevs.indexOf(a) !== i);
  2487 |     if (dup) return `Duplicate team abbreviation: ${dup}`;
  2488 |     return "";

  2492 |   const handleConfirmSave = async () => {

  2532 |     const cleanOne = (list) =>

  2535 |         const identityAbbrev = normalizeAbbrev(t.teamIdentity?.abbr || "");
  2536 |         const smartAbbrev = identityAbbrev || makeUniqueTeamAbbrev(t.id, label || t.label, list);
  2537 |         const abbrev = normalizeAbbrev(smartAbbrev || t.abbrev || "");

  2543 |       });
  2544 | 
  2545 |     // Save from the exact teams currently shown in Squad Shape Preview.
  2546 |     // This prevents stale hidden/local state from overwriting preview edits.

  2548 |       isFiveVFive ? localLeagueTeams : sourceTeams

  2550 | 
  2551 |     const cleanedFiveVFiveTeams = cleanOne(

  2556 |       validateTeams(cleanedLeagueTeams) || validateTeams(cleanedFiveVFiveTeams);
  2557 |     if (validationError) {
  2558 |       setSaveError(validationError);

  2561 | 
  2562 |     const newCaptainIds = new Set(
  2563 |       [...cleanedLeagueTeams, ...cleanedFiveVFiveTeams]
  2564 |         .map((t) => t.captainId)
  2565 |         .filter(Boolean)
  2566 |     );
  2567 | 
  2568 |     const currentCaptainIds = new Set(
  2569 |       allPlayers.filter((p) => p.roles?.captain === true).map((p) => p.id)

  2572 |     const toMakeCaptain = [...newCaptainIds].filter((id) => !currentCaptainIds.has(id));
  2573 |     const toRemoveCaptain = [...currentCaptainIds].filter((id) => !newCaptainIds.has(id));
  2574 | 
  2575 |     try {
  2576 |       const batch = writeBatch(db);
  2577 |       for (const pid of toMakeCaptain) {

  2580 |           {
  2581 |             "roles.captain": true,
  2582 |             "roles.player": true,
  2583 |             updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  2584 |           },
  2585 |           { merge: true }
  2586 |         );
  2587 |       }
  2588 | 
  2589 |       for (const pid of toRemoveCaptain) {
  2590 |         batch.set(
  2591 |           getPlayerDoc(db, pid, activeClubId),
  2592 |           {
  2593 |             "roles.captain": false,
  2594 |             updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  2595 |           },
  2596 |           { merge: true }
  2597 |         );
  2598 |       }
  2599 | 
  2600 |       await batch.commit();
  2601 |     } catch (err) {
  2602 |       console.error("[Squads] Error updating captain roles:", err);
  2603 |       setSaveError("Could not update captain roles in the database.");
  2604 |       return;
  2605 |     }
  2606 | 
  2607 |     onUpdateTeams?.(

  2027 |   const baseAbbrevFromName = (name) => {
  2028 |     const cleanName = String(name || "").trim();
  2029 |     const upper = cleanName.toUpperCase();
  2030 |     const words = upper.replace(/[^A-Z0-9 ]/g, " ").split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  2031 | 
  2032 |     if (!words.length) return "";
  2033 | 
  2034 |     const compact = words.join("");
  2035 | 
  2036 |     if (/^DARK/.test(compact)) return "DARK";
  2037 |     if (/^LIGHT/.test(compact)) return "LIGT";
  2038 | 
  2039 |     if (words[0] === "MAN" || words[0] === "MANCHESTER") {
  2040 |       const second = words[1] || "";
  2041 |       if (second.startsWith("C")) return "MCI";
  2042 |       if (second.startsWith("U")) return "MUN";
  2043 |     }
  2044 | 
  2045 |     if (compact.startsWith("MADRID")) return "MAD";
  2046 |     if (compact.startsWith("BARCELONA")) return "BAR";
  2047 |     if (compact.startsWith("AJAX")) return "AJA";
  2048 | 
  2049 |     if (words.length >= 2) {
  2050 |       const initials = words.map((word) => word[0]).join("");
  2051 |       if (initials.length >= 3) return initials.slice(0, 4);
  2052 |     }
  2053 | 
  2054 |     return compact.slice(0, compact.length >= 4 ? 4 : 3);
  2055 |   };
  2056 | 
  2057 |   const makeUniqueTeamAbbrev = (teamId, nextName, teamsList = sourceTeams) => {
  2058 |     const preferred = normalizeAbbrev(baseAbbrevFromName(nextName));
  2059 |     if (!preferred) return "";
  2060 | 
  2061 |     const taken = new Set(
  2062 |       (teamsList || [])
  2063 |         .filter((team) => team.id !== teamId)
  2064 |         .map((team) => normalizeAbbrev(team.abbrev || baseAbbrevFromName(team.label)))
  2065 |         .filter(Boolean)
  2066 |     );
  2067 | 
  2068 |     if (!taken.has(preferred)) return preferred;
  2069 | 
  2070 |     const compact = String(nextName || "").toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g, "");
  2071 |     const candidates = [
  2072 |       compact.slice(0, 4),
  2073 |       compact.slice(0, 3),
  2074 |       ...compact.split("").map((_, index) => `${preferred.slice(0, 3)}${compact[index] || ""}`),
  2075 |     ]
  2076 |       .map(normalizeAbbrev)
  2077 |       .filter((value) => value.length >= 3 && value.length <= 4);
  2078 | 
  2079 |     return candidates.find((candidate) => candidate && !taken.has(candidate)) || preferred;
  2080 |   };

    18 | function isChallengerChatActive(fixture = {}) {
    19 |   const status = String(fixture?.status || "").toLowerCase();
    20 | 
    21 |   if (
    22 |     status.includes("cancel") ||
    23 |     status.includes("rejected") ||
    24 |     status.includes("declined")
    25 |   ) {
    26 |     return false;
    27 |   }
    28 | 
    29 |   const dateText = String(fixture?.proposedDate || "").trim();
    30 |   if (!dateText) return true;
    31 | 
    32 |   const kickoffText = String(fixture?.proposedKickoff || "23:59").trim();
    33 |   const timeText = /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(kickoffText) ? kickoffText : "23:59";
    34 |   const fixtureTime = new Date(`${dateText}T${timeText}:00`);
    35 | 
    36 |   if (Number.isNaN(fixtureTime.getTime())) return true;
    37 | 
    38 |   return fixtureTime.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 >= Date.now();
    39 | }

   653 |     if (!isOwnMessage && !canAdminDelete) return;
   654 | 
   655 |     try {
   656 |       await deleteDoc(doc(db, "clubs", activeClubId, "chatMessages", message.id));
   657 |     } catch (err) {
   658 |       console.error("[ClubChatWidget] Failed deleting chat message:", err);
   659 |       window.alert("Could not delete this message just now.");
   660 |     }
   661 |   };
   662 | 
   663 |   const addChallengerChatEmoji = (emoji) => {
   664 |     setChallengerChatDraft((current) => `${current || ""}${emoji}`);
   665 |     setChallengerChatEmojiOpen(false);
   666 |   };
   667 | 
   668 |   const addClubChatEmoji = (emoji) => {
   669 |     setClubChatDraft((current) => `${current || ""}${emoji}`);
   670 |     setClubChatEmojiOpen(false);
   671 |   };
   672 | 
   673 |   const handleSendChallengerChatMessage = async () => {
   674 |     const text = String(challengerChatDraft || "").trim();
   675 | 
   676 |     if (!text || !canSendChallengerChat || !challengerChatFixture?.fixtureId) return;
   677 | 
   678 |     const senderName =
   679 |       selectedMember?.fullName ||
   680 |       selectedMember?.shortName ||
   681 |       currentUser?.displayName ||
   682 |       currentUser?.email?.split("@")[0] ||
   683 |       "Club member";
   684 | 
   685 |     try {
   686 |       await addDoc(collection(db, "clubChallengeFixtures", challengerChatFixture.fixtureId, "messages"), {
   687 |         type: "challenger_chat",

   689 |         fromClubId: activeClubId,
   690 |         fromClubName: activeClubName,
   691 |         senderName,
   692 |         senderUid: currentUser?.uid || "",
   693 |         createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
   694 |         createdAtMs: Date.now(),
   695 |       });
   696 | 
   697 |       setChallengerChatDraft("");
   698 |       setChallengerChatEmojiOpen(false);
   699 |     } catch (err) {
   700 |       console.error("[ClubChatWidget] Failed sending challenger chat message:", err);
   701 |       window.alert("Could not send this challenger chat message just now.");
   702 |     }
   703 |   };
   704 | 
   705 |   const handleSendClubChatMessage = async () => {
   706 |     const text = String(clubChatDraft || "").trim();
   707 |     if ((!text && !selectedHighlight) || !canSendClubChat) return;
   708 | 
   709 |     const senderName =
   710 |       selectedMember?.fullName ||
   711 |       selectedMember?.shortName ||
   712 |       currentUser?.displayName ||
   713 |       currentUser?.email?.split("@")[0] ||
   714 |       "Club member";
   715 | 
   716 |     const senderRole = isAdminViewer
   717 |       ? "admin"
   718 |       : selectedMember?.role || identity?.role || "player";
   719 |

   721 |       await addDoc(collection(db, "clubs", activeClubId, "chatMessages"), {
   722 |         text: text || "Shared a club highlight",
   723 |         senderName,
   724 |         senderRole,
   725 |         senderEmail: currentUser?.email || selectedMember?.email || identity?.email || "",

   737 |         ...(selectedHighlight ? {
   738 |           attachmentType: "highlight",

   744 |           highlightType: selectedHighlight.type || "highlight",
   745 |           highlightScorerName: selectedHighlight.scorerName || selectedHighlight.playerName || "",
   746 |           highlightTeamName: selectedHighlight.teamName || "",
   747 |           highlightLabel: selectedHighlight.label || selectedHighlight.title || "Goal clip",
   748 |         } : {}),
   749 |         createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
   750 |         createdAtMs: Date.now(),
   751 |       });
   752 | 
   753 |       setClubChatDraft("");
   754 |       setSelectedMentions([]);
   755 |       setSelectedHighlight(null);
   756 |       setReplyTarget(null);
   757 |       setHighlightPickerOpen(false);
   758 |       setClubChatEmojiOpen(false);
   759 |     } catch (err) {
   760 |       console.error("[ClubChatWidget] Failed sending club chat message:", err);
   761 |       window.alert("Could not send this club chat message just now.");
   762 |     }
   763 |   };
   764 | 
   765 |   const clubChatLatestMessageMs = clubChatMessages.reduce(
   766 |     (latest, message) => Math.max(latest, Number(message.createdAtMs || 0)),
   767 |     0
   768 |   );
   769 | 
   770 |   const clubChatUnreadCount = clubChatMessages.filter(
   771 |     (message) =>
   772 |       Number(message.createdAtMs || 0) > Number(clubChatLastSeenMs || 0) &&
   773 |       String(message.senderUid || "") !== String(currentUser?.uid || "")
   774 |   ).length;
   775 | 
   776 |   const challengerChatLatestMessageMs = challengerChatMessages.reduce(
   777 |     (latest, message) => Math.max(latest, Number(message.createdAtMs || 0)),
   778 |     0
   779 |   );
   780 | 
   781 |   const challengerChatUnreadCount = challengerChatMessages.filter(
   782 |     (message) =>
   783 |       Number(message.createdAtMs || 0) > Number(challengerChatLastSeenMs || 0) &&
   784 |       String(message.senderUid || "") !== String(currentUser?.uid || "")
   785 |   ).length;
   786 | 
   787 |   const totalChatUnreadCount = clubChatUnreadCount + challengerChatUnreadCount;
   788 |   const showChatToast = ({ room, senderName, text }) => {
   789 |     if (!isLauncherOnly || clubChatOpen) return;
   790 | 
   791 |     window.clearTimeout(toastTimerRef.current);
   792 | 
   793 |     setChatToast({
   794 |       room,
   795 |       senderName: senderName || "Club member",
   796 |       text: text || "New chat message",
   797 |     });
   798 | 
   799 |     try {
   800 |       navigator.vibrate?.(120);
   801 |     } catch {
   802 |       // Vibration is optional.
   803 |     }
   804 | 
   805 |     toastTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
   806 |       setChatToast(null);
   807 |     }, 4200);
   808 |   };
   809 | 
   810 |   useEffect(() => {
   811 |     if (!clubChatMessages.length) return;
   812 | 
   813 |     const latestMessage = clubChatMessages[clubChatMessages.length - 1];
   814 |     const latestMs = Number(latestMessage?.createdAtMs || 0);
   815 | 
   816 |     if (!latestMs) return;
   817 | 
   818 |     if (!latestNotifiedRef.current.club) {
   819 |       latestNotifiedRef.current.club = latestMs;
   820 |       return;
   821 |     }
   822 | 
   823 |     if (
   824 |       latestMs > latestNotifiedRef.current.club &&
   825 |       String(latestMessage?.senderUid || "") !== String(currentUser?.uid || "")
   826 |     ) {
   827 |       showChatToast({
   828 |         room: "club",
   829 |         senderName: latestMessage?.senderName,
   830 |         text: latestMessage?.text,
   831 |       });
   832 |     }
   833 | 
   834 |     latestNotifiedRef.current.club = latestMs;
   835 |   }, [clubChatMessages.length, currentUser?.uid, clubChatOpen, isLauncherOnly]);
   836 | 
   837 |   useEffect(() => {
   838 |     if (!challengerChatMessages.length) return;
   839 | 
   840 |     const latestMessage = challengerChatMessages[challengerChatMessages.length - 1];
   841 |     const latestMs = Number(latestMessage?.createdAtMs || 0);
   842 | 
   843 |     if (!latestMs) return;
   844 | 
   845 |     if (!latestNotifiedRef.current.challenger) {
   846 |       latestNotifiedRef.current.challenger = latestMs;
   847 |       return;
   848 |     }
   849 | 
   850 |     if (
   851 |       latestMs > latestNotifiedRef.current.challenger &&
   852 |       String(latestMessage?.senderUid || "") !== String(currentUser?.uid || "")
   853 |     ) {
   854 |       showChatToast({
   855 |         room: "challenger",
   856 |         senderName: latestMessage?.senderName || latestMessage?.fromClubName,
   857 |         text: latestMessage?.text || latestMessage?.message,
   858 |       });
   859 |     }
   860 | 
   861 |     latestNotifiedRef.current.challenger = latestMs;
   862 |   }, [challengerChatMessages.length, currentUser?.uid, clubChatOpen, isLauncherOnly]);
   863 | 
   864 | 
   865 | 
   866 |   useEffect(() => {
   867 |     if (activeChatRoom !== "challenger" || !challengerChatFixture?.fixtureId || !challengerChatLatestMessageMs) return;
   868 | 
   869 |     try {
   870 |       window.localStorage.setItem(
   871 |         `fanm_challenger_chat_seen_${challengerChatFixture.fixtureId}`,
   872 |         String(challengerChatLatestMessageMs)
   873 |       );
   874 |     } catch {
   875 |       // localStorage is optional
   876 |     }
   877 | 
   878 |     setChallengerChatLastSeenMs(challengerChatLatestMessageMs);
   879 |   }, [activeChatRoom, challengerChatFixture?.fixtureId, challengerChatLatestMessageMs]);
   880 | 
   881 |   useEffect(() => {
   882 |     if (!clubChatOpen || !activeClubId || !clubChatLatestMessageMs) return;
   883 | 
   884 |     try {
   885 |       window.localStorage.setItem(
   886 |         `fanm_club_chat_seen_${activeClubId}`,
   887 |         String(clubChatLatestMessageMs)
   888 |       );
   889 |     } catch {
   890 |       // localStorage is optional
   891 |     }
   892 | 
   893 |     setClubChatLastSeenMs(clubChatLatestMessageMs);
   894 |   }, [clubChatOpen, activeClubId, clubChatLatestMessageMs]);
   895 | 
   896 |   useEffect(() => {
   897 |     if (!isLauncherOnly || !clubChatOpen) return;
   898 | 
   899 |     try {
   900 |       window.history.pushState(
   901 |         { ...(window.history.state || {}), fanmClubChatOpen: true },
   902 |         "",
   903 |         window.location.href
   904 |       );
   905 |     } catch {
   906 |       // Browser history is optional.
   907 |     }
   908 | 
   909 |     const handlePhoneBack = () => {
   910 |       setClubChatOpen(false);
   911 |     };
   912 | 
   913 |     window.addEventListener("popstate", handlePhoneBack);
   914 | 
   915 |     return () => {
   916 |       window.removeEventListener("popstate", handlePhoneBack);
   917 |     };
   918 |   }, [isLauncherOnly, clubChatOpen]);
   919 | 
   920 |   useEffect(() => {
   921 |     if (clubChatOpen) {
   922 |       setClubChatTeaseOpen(false);
   923 |       return;
   924 |     }
   925 | 
   926 |     let idleTimer;
   927 |     let shrinkTimer;
   928 | 
   929 |     const startIdleTimer = () => {
   930 |       setClubChatTeaseOpen(false);
   931 |       window.clearTimeout(idleTimer);
   932 |       window.clearTimeout(shrinkTimer);
   933 | 
   934 |       idleTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
   935 |         setClubChatTeaseOpen(true);
   936 | 
   937 |         shrinkTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
   938 |           setClubChatTeaseOpen(false);
   939 |         }, 2000);
   940 |       }, 7000);
   941 |     };
   942 | 
   943 |     startIdleTimer();
   944 | 
   945 |     const activityEvents = ["click", "keydown", "mousemove", "touchstart", "scroll"];
   946 |     activityEvents.forEach((eventName) => {
   947 |       window.addEventListener(eventName, startIdleTimer, { passive: true });
   948 |     });
   949 | 
   950 |     return () => {
   951 |       window.clearTimeout(idleTimer);
   952 |       window.clearTimeout(shrinkTimer);
   953 |       activityEvents.forEach((eventName) => {
   954 |         window.removeEventListener(eventName, startIdleTimer);
   955 |       });
   956 |     };
   957 |   }, [clubChatOpen]);
   958 | 
   959 |   return (
   960 |     <>
   961 |       {chatToast && !clubChatOpen && (
   962 |         <button
   963 |           type="button"
   964 |           className="fanm-club-chat-toast"
   965 |           onClick={() => {
   966 |             setActiveChatRoom(chatToast.room || "club");
   967 |             setClubChatOpen(true);
   968 |             setChatToast(null);
   969 |           }}
   970 |         >
   971 |           <span>💬</span>
   972 |           <strong>{chatToast.senderName}</strong>
   973 |           <small>{chatToast.text}</small>
   974 |         </button>
   975 |       )}
   976 | 
   977 |       {isLauncherOnly && clubChatOpen && (
   978 |         <button
   979 |           type="button"
   980 |           className="fanm-club-chat-modal-backdrop"
   981 |           aria-label="Close club chat"
   982 |           onClick={() => setClubChatOpen(false)}
   983 |         />
   984 |       )}
   985 | 
   986 |       <section
   987 |       className={`card fanm-club-chat-card ${clubChatOpen ? "is-open" : "is-collapsed"} ${isLauncherOnly && clubChatOpen ? "is-modal-open" : ""} ${isLauncherOnly && !clubChatOpen && launcherIsIdle ? "is-idle" : ""} ${isLauncherOnly && launcherIsDragging ? "is-dragging" : ""} ${
   988 |         !clubChatOpen && clubChatTeaseOpen ? "is-teasing" : ""
   989 |       }`}
   990 |       style={{
   991 |         ...premiumPanelStyle,

   995 |       <button

  1000 |         onClick={() => {
  1001 |           if (isLauncherOnly) {
  1002 |             setClubChatOpen(true);
  1003 |             return;
  1004 |           }
  1005 |           setClubChatOpen((current) => !current);
  1006 |         }}
  1007 |       >
  1008 |         <span className="fanm-club-chat-launcher-icon">💬</span>
  1009 | 
  1010 |         <span className="fanm-club-chat-launcher-text">
  1011 |           <strong>{activeClubName} Chat</strong>

  1013 |             {clubChatMessages.length
  1014 |               ? `${clubChatMessages.length} club message${clubChatMessages.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`
  1015 |               : "Private club room"}
  1016 |           </small>
  1017 |         </span>
  1018 | 
  1019 |         <span className="fanm-club-chat-header-actions">
  1020 |           {totalChatUnreadCount > 0 ? (
  1021 |             <span className="fanm-club-chat-unread">{totalChatUnreadCount}</span>
  1022 |           ) : (
  1023 |             <span className="fanm-club-chat-live-pill">Live</span>
  1024 |           )}
  1025 | 
  1026 |           {isLauncherOnly && clubChatOpen ? (
  1027 |             <span
  1028 |               className="fanm-club-chat-minimize-icon"
  1029 |               onClick={(event) => {
  1030 |                 event.stopPropagation();
  1031 |                 setClubChatOpen(false);
  1032 |               }}
  1033 |               title="Minimize chat"
  1034 |             >
  1035 |               −
  1036 |             </span>
  1037 |           ) : null}
  1038 |         </span>
  1039 |       </button>
  1040 | 
  1041 |       {clubChatOpen && (
  1042 |         <>
  1043 |           <div className="fanm-club-chat-head">

  1045 |               <h2>
  1046 |                 {activeChatRoom === "challenger"
  1047 |                   ? "Challenger Chat"
  1048 |                   : `${activeClubName} Chat`}
  1049 |               </h2>
  1050 |               <p className="muted small">
  1051 |                 {activeChatRoom === "challenger"
  1052 |                   ? `Match chat with ${challengerChatOpponentName}. This room is available only while the fixture is active.`
  1053 |                   : "Private messages for approved club members, captains and admins."}
  1054 |               </p>
  1055 |             </div>
  1056 |           </div>
  1057 | 
  1058 |           {challengerChatFixture?.fixtureId ? (
  1059 |             <div className="fanm-chat-room-switcher fanm-chat-room-switcher--compact">
  1060 |               {activeChatRoom === "club" ? (
  1061 |                 <button
  1062 |                   type="button"
  1063 |                   className="fanm-chat-room-tab fanm-chat-room-tab--challenger"

  1072 |                     </small>
  1073 |                   </div>
  1074 |                   {challengerChatUnreadCount > 0 ? <em>{challengerChatUnreadCount}</em> : null}

  1076 |               ) : (
  1077 |                 <button
  1078 |                   type="button"
  1079 |                   className="fanm-chat-room-tab"
  1080 |                   onClick={() => setActiveChatRoom("club")}
  1081 |                 >
  1082 |                   <span>💬</span>

  1084 |                     <strong>Club Chat</strong>
  1085 |                     <small>Back to private club room</small>

  1092 | 
  1093 |           {activeChatRoom === "club" ? (

  1096 |                 clubChatMessages.map((message, index) => {
  1097 |                   const previousMessage = clubChatMessages[index - 1];
  1098 |                   const currentDateLabel = getChatDateLabel(message.createdAtMs);
  1099 |                   const previousDateLabel = getChatDateLabel(previousMessage?.createdAtMs);
  1100 |                   const showDateChip = currentDateLabel && currentDateLabel !== previousDateLabel;
  1101 | 
  1102 |                   const currentViewerKeys = [
  1103 |                     currentUser?.uid,
  1104 |                     currentUser?.email,
  1105 |                     selectedMember?.email,
  1106 |                     identity?.email,
  1107 |                     selectedMember?.fullName,
  1108 |                     selectedMember?.shortName,
  1109 |                     identity?.fullName,
  1110 |                     identity?.shortName,
  1111 |                     currentUser?.displayName,
  1112 |                   ]
  1113 |                     .map((value) => String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase())
  1114 |                     .filter(Boolean);
  1115 |

  1117 |                     message.senderUid,
  1118 |                     message.senderEmail,
  1119 |                     message.senderName,
  1120 |                   ]
  1121 |                     .map((value) => String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase())
  1122 |                     .filter(Boolean);
  1123 | 
  1124 |                   const mine = messageSenderKeys.some((key) => currentViewerKeys.includes(key));
  1125 | 
  1126 |                   const isAdminMessage =
  1127 |                     String(message.senderRole || "").toLowerCase().includes("admin") ||
  1128 |                     String(message.senderRole || "").toLowerCase().includes("captain");
  1129 | 
  1130 |                   const previousSenderKeys = [
  1131 |                     previousMessage?.senderUid,
  1132 |                     previousMessage?.senderEmail,
  1133 |                     previousMessage?.senderName,
  1134 |                   ]
  1135 |                     .map((value) => String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase())
  1136 |                     .filter(Boolean);

  1139 |                     previousSenderKeys.length > 0 &&
  1140 |                     messageSenderKeys.some((key) => previousSenderKeys.includes(key));
  1141 |

  1143 |                     previousMessage?.createdAtMs && message.createdAtMs
  1144 |                       ? Math.abs(Number(message.createdAtMs) - Number(previousMessage.createdAtMs)) / 60000
  1145 |                       : 999;
  1146 | 
  1147 |                   const groupedWithPrevious =
  1148 |                     !showDateChip &&
  1149 |                     sameSenderAsPrevious &&
  1150 |                     minutesSincePrevious <= 5 &&

  1152 |                     !previousMessage?.replyToId &&
  1153 |                     !message.attachmentType &&
  1154 |                     !previousMessage?.attachmentType;

  1160 |                       ) : null}
  1161 |

  1169 |                         </div>

  1171 |                       {message.replyToId ? (

  1177 |

  1179 |                         <button
  1180 |                           type="button"

  1190 |                               type: message.highlightType || "highlight",
  1191 |                             });
  1192 |                           }}

  1196 |                               src={message.highlightMediaUrl}

  1198 |                               playsInline

  1205 |                           <div>

  1211 |                                 message.highlightTeamName || "",

  1213 |                             </small>
  1214 |                           </div>
  1215 |                         </button>
  1216 |                       ) : null}
  1217 |                       <p>{renderMessageTextWithMentions(message.text)}</p>
  1218 | 
  1219 |                       <div className="fanm-club-chat-message-footer fanm-club-chat-message-footer--compact">
  1220 |                         <div className="fanm-chat-reaction-row">
  1221 |                         {getVisibleReactions(message)
  1222 |                           .map((emoji) => {
  1223 |                             const count = getReactionUsers(message, emoji).length;
  1224 |                             return (
  1225 |                               <button
  1226 |                                 type="button"

    78 | function fixtureTimestamp(fixture = {}) {
    79 |   const date = cleanText(
    80 |     fixture?.confirmedDate ||
    81 |       fixture?.proposedDate ||
    82 |       fixture?.matchDate
    83 |   );
    84 | 
    85 |   const kickoff = cleanText(
    86 |     fixture?.confirmedKickoff ||
    87 |       fixture?.proposedKickoff ||
    88 |       fixture?.kickoff,
    89 |     "00:00"
    90 |   );
    91 | 
    92 |   if (!date) {
    93 |     return Number(fixture?.createdAtMs || fixture?.updatedAtMs || 0);
    94 |   }
    95 | 
    96 |   const parsed = new Date(`${date}T${kickoff}`);
    97 | 
    98 |   return Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())
    99 |     ? Number(fixture?.createdAtMs || fixture?.updatedAtMs || 0)
   100 |     : parsed.getTime();
   101 | }
   102 | 
   103 | function formatFixtureDate(fixture = {}) {
   104 |   const date = cleanText(
   105 |     fixture?.confirmedDate ||
   106 |       fixture?.proposedDate ||
   107 |       fixture?.matchDate
   108 |   );
   109 | 
   110 |   if (!date) return "Date to be confirmed";
   111 | 
   112 |   const parsed = new Date(`${date}T12:00:00`);
   113 | 
   114 |   if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) return date;
   115 | 
   116 |   return parsed.toLocaleDateString("en-ZA", {
   117 |     weekday: "short",
   118 |     day: "numeric",
   119 |     month: "short",
   120 |     year: "numeric",
   121 |   });
   122 | }

   142 | function challengePlayersPerSide(format = "") {
   143 |   const value = String(format || "").trim().toUpperCase();
   144 | 
   145 |   if (["11V11", "11_V_11", "11_ASIDE"].includes(value)) return 11;
   146 |   if (["7V7", "7_V_7", "7_ASIDE"].includes(value)) return 7;
   147 |   if (["6V6", "6_V_6", "6_ASIDE"].includes(value)) return 6;
   148 |   return 5;
   149 | }
