   217 |     club?.clubName ||
   218 |       club?.name ||
   219 |       club?.displayName ||
   220 |       club?.shortName,
   221 |     "Unnamed club"
   222 |   );
   223 | }
   224 | 
   225 | function challengeClubLogo(club = {}) {
   226 |   return cleanText(
   227 |     club?.logoUrl ||
   228 |       club?.clubLogoUrl ||
   229 |       club?.image ||
   230 |       club?.badgeUrl
   231 |   );
   232 | }
   233 | 
   234 | function tomorrowDateValue() {
   235 |   const value = new Date();
   236 |   value.setDate(value.getDate() + 1);
   237 |   return value.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
   238 | }
   239 | 
   240 | function freshPairingDraft() {
   241 |   return {
   242 |     homeClubId: "",
   243 |     awayClubId: "",
   244 |     format: "5v5",
   245 |     proposedDate: tomorrowDateValue(),
   246 |     proposedKickoff: "18:30",
   247 |     venue: "",
   248 |     message: "",
   249 |   };
   250 | }
   251 | 
   252 | function ClubBadge({ name, logo }) {
   253 |   return (
   254 |     <span className="challenge-workspace-fixture__badge">
   255 |       {logo ? (
   256 |         <img src={logo} alt="" />
   257 |       ) : (
   258 |         <strong>{clubInitials(name)}</strong>
   259 |       )}
   260 |     </span>
   261 |   );
   262 | }
   263 | 
   264 | export default function ChallengeCentrePage({
   265 |   identity = null,
   266 |   onBack,
   267 |   onOpenFixture,
   268 |   onOpenMatchControl,
   269 |   onOpenMatchReport,
   270 | }) {
   271 |   const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState(null);
   272 |   const [clubs, setClubs] = useState([]);
   273 |   const [fixtures, setFixtures] = useState([]);
   274 |   const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState("upcoming");
   275 |   const [selectedFixtureId, setSelectedFixtureId] = useState("");
   276 |   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   277 |   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   278 |   const [showPairClubsModal, setShowPairClubsModal] = useState(false);
   279 |   const [pairingDraft, setPairingDraft] = useState(
   280 |     freshPairingDraft
   281 |   );
   282 |   const [pairingSaving, setPairingSaving] = useState(false);
   283 |   const [pairingError, setPairingError] = useState("");
   284 |   const [pairingSuccess, setPairingSuccess] = useState("");
   285 | 
   286 | 
   287 |   useEffect(() => {
   288 |     return onAuthStateChanged(auth, (user) => {
   289 |       setCurrentUser(user || null);
   290 |     });
   291 |   }, []);
   292 | 
   293 |   useEffect(() => {
   294 |     let cancelled = false;
   295 | 
   296 |     async function loadCentreData() {
   297 |       try {
   298 |         setLoading(true);
   299 |         setError("");
   300 | 
   301 |         const [clubsSnap, fixturesSnap] = await Promise.all([
   302 |           getDocs(collection(db, "clubs")),
   303 |           getDocs(collection(db, "clubChallengeFixtures")),
   304 |         ]);
   305 | 
   306 |         if (cancelled) return;
   307 | 
   308 |         const loadedClubs = clubsSnap.docs.map(
   309 |           (clubDoc) => ({
   310 |             id: clubDoc.id,
   311 |             ...clubDoc.data(),
   312 |           })
   313 |         );
   314 | 
   315 |         const loadedFixtures = fixturesSnap.docs.map(
   316 |           (fixtureDoc) => ({
   317 |             id: fixtureDoc.id,
   318 |             ...fixtureDoc.data(),
   319 |           })
   320 |         );
   321 | 
   322 |         const currentEmail = normalizeEmail(
   323 |           auth.currentUser?.email
   324 |         );
   325 | 
   326 |         const canRepairPlatformPairings =
   327 |           SUPER_ADMIN_EMAILS.includes(currentEmail);
   328 | 
   329 |         const prematurePlatformFixtures =
   330 |           canRepairPlatformPairings
   331 |             ? loadedFixtures.filter((fixture) => {
   332 |                 const source = cleanText(fixture?.source);
   333 |                 const status = fixtureStatus(fixture);
   334 | 
   335 |                 const homeAccepted = Boolean(
   336 |                   fixture?.homeAccepted ||
   337 |                   fixture?.responses?.[fixture?.homeClubId] ===
   338 |                     "accepted"
   339 |                 );
   340 | 
   341 |                 const awayAccepted = Boolean(
   342 |                   fixture?.awayAccepted ||
   343 |                   fixture?.responses?.[fixture?.awayClubId] ===
   344 |                     "accepted"
   345 |                 );
   346 | 
   347 |                 return (
   348 |                   source === "platform_pairing" &&
   349 |                   ["awaiting_responses", "pending"].includes(status) &&
   350 |                   !(homeAccepted && awayAccepted)
   351 |                 );
   352 |               })
   353 |             : [];
   354 | 
   355 |         if (prematurePlatformFixtures.length) {
   356 |           const repairBatch = writeBatch(db);
   357 | 
   358 |           prematurePlatformFixtures.forEach((fixture) => {
   359 |             const fixtureId = cleanText(
   360 |               fixture?.fixtureId || fixture?.id
   361 |             );
   362 | 
   363 |             const pairingId = cleanText(
   364 |               fixture?.platformPairingId ||
   365 |               fixture?.challengeId ||
   366 |               fixtureId.replace(/^challenge_/, ""),
   367 |               `platform_pairing_${Date.now()}`
   368 |             );
   369 | 
   370 |             const homeClubId = cleanText(fixture?.homeClubId);
   371 |             const awayClubId = cleanText(fixture?.awayClubId);
   372 | 
   373 |             if (!fixtureId || !homeClubId || !awayClubId) {
   374 |               return;
   375 |             }
   376 | 
   377 |             const pendingPayload = {
   378 |               ...fixture,
   379 | 
   380 |               challengeId: pairingId,
   381 |               platformPairingId: pairingId,
   382 | 
   383 |               source: "platform_pairing",
   384 |               platformInstigated: true,
   385 | 
   386 |               status: "pending",
   387 |               fixtureStatus: "awaiting_both_clubs",
   388 | 
   389 |               challengerClubId: homeClubId,
   390 |               challengerClubName:
   391 |                 fixture?.homeClubName || "Home Club",
   392 |               challengerClubLogo:
   393 |                 fixture?.homeClubLogo || "",
   394 | 
   395 |               targetClubId: awayClubId,
   396 |               targetClubName:
   397 |                 fixture?.awayClubName || "Away Club",
   398 |               targetClubLogo:
   399 |                 fixture?.awayClubLogo || "",
   400 | 
   401 |               homeAccepted: false,
   402 |               awayAccepted: false,
   403 |               acceptedClubIds: [],
   404 | 
   405 |               responses: {
   406 |                 [homeClubId]: "pending",
   407 |                 [awayClubId]: "pending",
   408 |               },
   409 | 
   410 |               repairedFromPrematureFixture: true,
   411 |               repairedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   412 |               repairedAtMs: Date.now(),
   413 | 
   414 |               updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   415 |               updatedAtMs: Date.now(),
   416 |             };
   417 | 
   418 |             repairBatch.set(
   419 |               doc(db, "clubChallenges", pairingId),
   420 |               pendingPayload,
   421 |               { merge: true }
   422 |             );
   423 | 
   424 |             repairBatch.set(
   425 |               doc(
   426 |                 db,
   427 |                 "clubs",
   428 |                 homeClubId,
   429 |                 "incomingChallenges",
   430 |                 pairingId
   431 |               ),
   432 |               {
   433 |                 ...pendingPayload,
   434 |                 receivingClubId: homeClubId,
   435 |                 opponentClubId: awayClubId,
   436 |                 opponentClubName:
   437 |                   fixture?.awayClubName || "Away Club",
   438 |                 opponentClubLogo:
   439 |                   fixture?.awayClubLogo || "",
   440 |               },
   441 |               { merge: true }
   442 |             );
   443 | 
   444 |             repairBatch.set(
   445 |               doc(
   446 |                 db,
   447 |                 "clubs",
   448 |                 awayClubId,
   449 |                 "incomingChallenges",
   450 |                 pairingId
   451 |               ),
   452 |               {
   453 |                 ...pendingPayload,
   454 |                 receivingClubId: awayClubId,
   455 |                 opponentClubId: homeClubId,
   456 |                 opponentClubName:
   457 |                   fixture?.homeClubName || "Home Club",
   458 |                 opponentClubLogo:
   459 |                   fixture?.homeClubLogo || "",
   460 |               },
   461 |               { merge: true }
   462 |             );
   463 | 
   464 |             repairBatch.delete(
   465 |               doc(db, "clubChallengeFixtures", fixtureId)
   466 |             );
   467 | 
   468 |             repairBatch.delete(
   469 |               doc(
   470 |                 db,
   471 |                 "clubs",
   472 |                 homeClubId,
   473 |                 "fixtures",
   474 |                 fixtureId
   475 |               )
   476 |             );
   477 | 
   478 |             repairBatch.delete(
   479 |               doc(
   480 |                 db,
   481 |                 "clubs",
   482 |                 awayClubId,
   483 |                 "fixtures",
   484 |                 fixtureId
   485 |               )
   486 |             );
   487 |           });
   488 | 
   489 |           await repairBatch.commit();
   490 |         }
   491 | 
   492 |         const prematureIds = new Set(
   493 |           prematurePlatformFixtures.map((fixture) =>
   494 |             String(fixture?.fixtureId || fixture?.id)
   495 |           )
   496 |         );
   497 | 
   498 |         setClubs(loadedClubs);
   499 | 
   500 |         setFixtures(
   501 |           loadedFixtures.filter(
   502 |             (fixture) =>
   503 |               !prematureIds.has(
   504 |                 String(fixture?.fixtureId || fixture?.id)
   505 |               )
   506 |           )
   507 |         );
   508 |       } catch (loadError) {
   509 |         console.error(
   510 |           "[ChallengeCentrePage] Could not load challenge centre:",
   511 |           loadError
   512 |         );
   513 | 
   514 |         if (!cancelled) {
   515 |           setError("Challenge fixtures could not be loaded right now.");
   516 |         }
   517 |       } finally {
   518 |         if (!cancelled) {
   519 |           setLoading(false);
   520 |         }
   521 |       }
   522 |     }
   523 | 
   524 |     loadCentreData();
   525 | 
   526 |     return () => {
   527 |       cancelled = true;
   528 |     };
   529 |   }, []);
   530 | 
   531 | 
   532 |   /* FANM PLATFORM PAIRING ACTIONS */
   533 | 
   534 |   const openPairClubsModal = () => {
   535 |     setPairingDraft(freshPairingDraft());
   536 |     setPairingError("");
   537 |     setPairingSuccess("");
   538 |     setShowPairClubsModal(true);
   539 |   };
   540 | 
   541 |   const closePairClubsModal = () => {
   542 |     if (pairingSaving) return;
   543 |     setShowPairClubsModal(false);
   544 |     setPairingError("");
   545 |     setPairingSuccess("");
   546 |   };
   547 | 
   548 |   const updatePairingDraft = (field, value) => {
   549 |     setPairingDraft((current) => ({
   550 |       ...current,
   551 |       [field]: value,
   552 |     }));
   553 | 
   554 |     setPairingError("");
   555 |     setPairingSuccess("");
   556 |   };
   557 | 
   558 |   const createPlatformPairedFixture = async () => {
   559 |     if (!isPlatformAdmin || pairingSaving) return;
   560 | 
   561 |     const homeClubId = cleanText(pairingDraft.homeClubId);
   562 |     const awayClubId = cleanText(pairingDraft.awayClubId);
   563 |     const proposedDate = cleanText(pairingDraft.proposedDate);
   564 |     const proposedKickoff = cleanText(pairingDraft.proposedKickoff);
   565 |     const venue = cleanText(pairingDraft.venue);
   566 |     const format = cleanText(pairingDraft.format, "5v5");
   567 | 
   568 |     if (!homeClubId || !awayClubId) {
   569 |       setPairingError("Select both participating clubs.");
   570 |       return;
   571 |     }
   572 | 
   573 |     if (homeClubId === awayClubId) {
   574 |       setPairingError("The home and away club must be different.");
   575 |       return;
   576 |     }
   577 | 
   578 |     if (!proposedDate || !proposedKickoff || !venue) {
   579 |       setPairingError(
   580 |         "Complete the date, kickoff time and venue."
   581 |       );
   582 |       return;
   583 |     }
   584 | 
   585 |     const homeClub = clubs.find(
   586 |       (club) =>
   587 |         String(club?.id || club?.clubId) === String(homeClubId)
   588 |     );
   589 | 
   590 |     const awayClub = clubs.find(
   591 |       (club) =>
   592 |         String(club?.id || club?.clubId) === String(awayClubId)
   593 |     );
   594 | 
   595 |     if (!homeClub || !awayClub) {
   596 |       setPairingError(
   597 |         "One of the selected clubs could not be found."
   598 |       );
   599 |       return;
   600 |     }
   601 | 
   602 |     const nowMs = Date.now();
   603 |     const pairingId = `platform_pairing_${nowMs}`;
   604 | 
   605 |     const homeClubName = challengeClubName(homeClub);
   606 |     const awayClubName = challengeClubName(awayClub);
   607 | 
   608 |     const requestPayload = {
   609 |       challengeId: pairingId,
   610 |       platformPairingId: pairingId,
   611 | 
   612 |       source: "platform_pairing",
   613 |       platformInstigated: true,
   614 | 
   615 |       status: "pending",
   616 |       fixtureStatus: "awaiting_both_clubs",
   617 | 
   618 |       challengerClubId: homeClubId,
   619 |       challengerClubName: homeClubName,
   620 |       challengerClubLogo: challengeClubLogo(homeClub),
   621 | 
   622 |       targetClubId: awayClubId,
   623 |       targetClubName: awayClubName,
   624 |       targetClubLogo: challengeClubLogo(awayClub),
   625 | 
   626 |       homeClubId,
   627 |       homeClubName,
   628 |       homeClubLogo: challengeClubLogo(homeClub),
   629 | 
   630 |       awayClubId,
   631 |       awayClubName,
   632 |       awayClubLogo: challengeClubLogo(awayClub),
   633 | 
   634 |       participatingClubIds: [homeClubId, awayClubId],
   635 | 
   636 |       format,
   637 |       proposedDate,
   638 |       proposedKickoff,
   639 |       venue,
   640 | 
   641 |       message: cleanText(pairingDraft.message),
   642 | 
   643 |       homeAccepted: false,
   644 |       awayAccepted: false,
   645 |       acceptedClubIds: [],
   646 | 
   647 |       responses: {
   648 |         [homeClubId]: "pending",
   649 |         [awayClubId]: "pending",
   650 |       },
   651 | 
   652 |       createdByRole: "platform_admin",
   653 |       createdByEmail: normalizeEmail(currentUser?.email),
   654 |       createdByName: cleanText(
   655 |         currentUser?.displayName,
   656 |         "5 Asides Near Me Admin"
   657 |       ),
   658 | 
   659 |       createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
   660 |       createdAtMs: nowMs,
   661 | 
   662 |       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   663 |       updatedAtMs: nowMs,
   664 |     };
   665 | 
   666 |     try {
   667 |       setPairingSaving(true);
   668 |       setPairingError("");
   669 |       setPairingSuccess("");
   670 | 
   671 |       const batch = writeBatch(db);
   672 | 
   673 |       // Central pending challenge record.
   674 |       batch.set(
   675 |         doc(db, "clubChallenges", pairingId),
   676 |         requestPayload,
   677 |         { merge: true }
   678 |       );
   679 | 
   680 |       // Both clubs receive the normal incoming-challenge popup.
   681 |       batch.set(
   682 |         doc(
   683 |           db,
   684 |           "clubs",
   685 |           homeClubId,
   686 |           "incomingChallenges",
   687 |           pairingId
   688 |         ),
   689 |         {
   690 |           ...requestPayload,
   691 |           receivingClubId: homeClubId,
   692 |           opponentClubId: awayClubId,
   693 |           opponentClubName: awayClubName,
   694 |           opponentClubLogo: challengeClubLogo(awayClub),
   695 |         },
   696 |         { merge: true }
   697 |       );
   698 | 
   699 |       batch.set(
   700 |         doc(
   701 |           db,
   702 |           "clubs",
   703 |           awayClubId,
   704 |           "incomingChallenges",
   705 |           pairingId
   706 |         ),
   707 |         {
   708 |           ...requestPayload,
   709 |           receivingClubId: awayClubId,
   710 |           opponentClubId: homeClubId,
   711 |           opponentClubName: homeClubName,
   712 |           opponentClubLogo: challengeClubLogo(homeClub),
   713 |         },
   714 |         { merge: true }
   715 |       );
   716 | 
   717 |       await batch.commit();
   718 | 
   719 |       setPairingSuccess(
   720 |         `Challenge invitations sent to ${homeClubName} and ${awayClubName}.`
   721 |       );
   722 | 
   723 |       window.setTimeout(() => {
   724 |         setShowPairClubsModal(false);
   725 |         setPairingSuccess("");
   726 |       }, 1100);
   727 |     } catch (createError) {
   728 |       console.error(
   729 |         "[ChallengeCentrePage] Could not pair clubs:",
   730 |         createError
   731 |       );
   732 | 
   733 |       setPairingError(
   734 |         "The challenge invitations could not be sent."
   735 |       );
   736 |     } finally {
   737 |       setPairingSaving(false);
   738 |     }
   739 |   };
   740 | 
   741 |   const isPlatformAdmin = SUPER_ADMIN_EMAILS.includes(
   742 |     normalizeEmail(currentUser?.email)
   743 |   );
   744 | 
   745 |   const managedClubIds = useMemo(() => {
   746 |     const ids = new Set();
   747 |     const email = normalizeEmail(currentUser?.email);
   748 | 
   749 |     [identity?.clubId, identity?.homeClubId, identity?.activeClubId]
   750 |       .map(cleanText)
   751 |       .filter(Boolean)
   752 |       .forEach((clubId) => ids.add(clubId));
   753 | 
   754 |     clubs.forEach((club) => {
   755 |       if (
   756 |         isPlatformAdmin ||
   757 |         (email && getClubAdminEmails(club).includes(email))
   758 |       ) {
   759 |         const clubId = cleanText(club?.id || club?.clubId);
   760 |         if (clubId) ids.add(clubId);
   761 |       }
   762 |     });
   763 | 
   764 |     return Array.from(ids);
   765 |   }, [
   766 |     clubs,
   767 |     currentUser?.email,
   768 |     identity?.clubId,
   769 |     identity?.homeClubId,
   770 |     identity?.activeClubId,
   771 |     isPlatformAdmin,
   772 |   ]);
   773 | 
   774 |   const relevantFixtures = useMemo(() => {
   775 |     if (isPlatformAdmin) return fixtures;
   776 | 
   777 |     const managedIds = new Set(managedClubIds.map(String));
   778 | 
   779 |     return fixtures.filter((fixture) => {
   780 |       const participantIds = [
   781 |         fixture?.homeClubId,
   782 |         fixture?.awayClubId,
   783 |         ...(Array.isArray(fixture?.participatingClubIds)
   784 |           ? fixture.participatingClubIds
   785 |           : []),
   786 |       ]
   787 |         .map(cleanText)
   788 |         .filter(Boolean);
   789 | 
   790 |       return participantIds.some((clubId) => managedIds.has(clubId));
   791 |     });
   792 |   }, [fixtures, managedClubIds, isPlatformAdmin]);
   793 | 
   794 |   const counts = useMemo(() => {
   795 |     return relevantFixtures.reduce(
   796 |       (result, fixture) => {
   797 |         if (isCompletedFixture(fixture)) {
   798 |           result.results += 1;
   799 |         } else if (isRequestFixture(fixture)) {
   800 |           result.requests += 1;
   801 |         } else {
   802 |           result.upcoming += 1;
   803 |         }
   804 | 
   805 |         if (isLiveFixture(fixture)) result.live += 1;
   806 | 
   807 |         return result;
   808 |       },
   809 |       { upcoming: 0, requests: 0, results: 0, live: 0 }
   810 |     );
   811 |   }, [relevantFixtures]);
   812 | 
   813 |   const visibleFixtures = useMemo(() => {
   814 |     return relevantFixtures
   815 |       .filter((fixture) => {
   816 |         if (activeView === "results") return isCompletedFixture(fixture);
   817 |         if (activeView === "requests") return isRequestFixture(fixture);
   818 | 
   819 |         return (
   820 |           !isCompletedFixture(fixture) &&
   821 |           !isRequestFixture(fixture)
   822 |         );
   823 |       })
   824 |       .sort((a, b) => {
   825 |         const timeA = fixtureTimestamp(a);
   826 |         const timeB = fixtureTimestamp(b);
   827 | 
   828 |         return activeView === "results"
   829 |           ? timeB - timeA
   830 |           : timeA - timeB;
   831 |       });
   832 |   }, [relevantFixtures, activeView]);
   833 | 
   834 |   const selectedFixture = useMemo(() => {
   835 |     return (
   836 |       visibleFixtures.find(
   837 |         (fixture) =>
   838 |           String(fixture?.fixtureId || fixture?.id) ===
   839 |           String(selectedFixtureId)
   840 |       ) ||
   841 |       visibleFixtures[0] ||
   842 |       null
   843 |     );
   844 |   }, [visibleFixtures, selectedFixtureId]);
   845 | 
   846 |   useEffect(() => {
   847 |     if (!visibleFixtures.length) {
   848 |       setSelectedFixtureId("");
   849 |       return;
   850 |     }
   851 | 
   852 |     const currentExists = visibleFixtures.some(
   853 |       (fixture) =>
   854 |         String(fixture?.fixtureId || fixture?.id) ===
   855 |         String(selectedFixtureId)
   856 |     );
   857 | 
   858 |     if (!currentExists) {
   859 |       setSelectedFixtureId(
   860 |         String(
   861 |           visibleFixtures[0]?.fixtureId ||
   862 |             visibleFixtures[0]?.id ||
   863 |             ""
   864 |         )
   865 |       );
   866 |     }
   867 |   }, [visibleFixtures, selectedFixtureId]);
   868 | 
   869 |   const requiredPlayers = challengePlayersPerSide(
   870 |     selectedFixture?.format || selectedFixture?.gameFormat
   871 |   );
   872 | 
   873 |   const homeAccepted = fixtureClubAccepted(selectedFixture, "home");
   874 |   const awayAccepted = fixtureClubAccepted(selectedFixture, "away");
   875 | 
   876 |   const homeSquadCount = fixtureSquadCount(selectedFixture, "home");
   877 |   const awaySquadCount = fixtureSquadCount(selectedFixture, "away");
   878 | 
   879 |   const homeSquadReady = homeSquadCount >= requiredPlayers;
   880 |   const awaySquadReady = awaySquadCount >= requiredPlayers;
   881 | 
   882 |   const refereeName = fixtureRefereeName(selectedFixture);
   883 |   const refereeEmail = fixtureRefereeEmail(selectedFixture);
   884 |   const refereeReady = Boolean(refereeEmail);
   885 | 
   886 |   const fixtureReady =
   887 |     Boolean(selectedFixture) &&
   888 |     homeAccepted &&
   889 |     awayAccepted &&
   890 |     homeSquadReady &&
   891 |     awaySquadReady;
   892 | 
   893 |   const fixtureLive = isLiveFixture(selectedFixture);
   894 |   const fixtureComplete = isCompletedFixture(selectedFixture);
   895 | 
   896 |   const homeName = cleanText(
   897 |     selectedFixture?.homeClubName ||
   898 |       selectedFixture?.challengerClubName,
   899 |     "Home Club"
   900 |   );
   901 | 
   902 |   const awayName = cleanText(
   903 |     selectedFixture?.awayClubName ||
   904 |       selectedFixture?.targetClubName,
   905 |     "Away Club"
   906 |   );
   907 | 
   908 |   const homeScore = Number.isFinite(Number(selectedFixture?.homeScore))
   909 |     ? Number(selectedFixture.homeScore)
   910 |     : 0;
   911 | 
   912 |   const awayScore = Number.isFinite(Number(selectedFixture?.awayScore))
   913 |     ? Number(selectedFixture.awayScore)
   914 |     : 0;
   915 | 
   916 |   return (
   917 |     <main className="challenge-ops-page">
   918 |       <div className="challenge-ops-layout">
   919 |         <aside className="challenge-ops-sidebar">
   920 |           <div className="challenge-ops-brand">
   921 |             <span>⚔</span>
   922 |             <div>
   923 |               <strong>Challenge Centre</strong>
   924 |               <small>Interclub football</small>
   925 |             </div>
   926 |           </div>
   927 |

  1595 |     </main>
  1596 |   );
  1597 | }
